# Jan–Feb 2027 scaling readiness research

Prepared 2026-11-21T02:22:30.744845+00:00; financial source cutoff November 20, 2026. Fixed population: the original 64 R1 companies, without replacing difficult formats.

**512 company-quarters reviewed; 472 source-matched, 40 blocked. 63 companies have at least six source-matched quarters. 11 companies have all four announcement/release pairs verified. Zero are operationally live-ready for 2027: future publication dates have not been accepted and none was imported.**

A source-matched quarter has a directly issued consolidated absolute-dollar revenue range, an exact compatible fiscal-period actual, a source-supported issuance/filing cutoff, a release reconciliation, and no identified unresolved exclusion in this audit. These are agent/source-curated research observations, not owner-reviewed production records or a claim of flawless semantic validation. Quotes and offsets locate evidence; they do not prove its interpretation. Before onboarding, review the source packets and company-specific scope/calendar/concept choices. The full corporate-scope and non-8-K disclosure search is not certified by this bounded SEC 8-K audit. Thus the counts are research-usable source pairs, not a completed production eligibility gate.

## Selection and dating

- Last eight distinct completed-quarter regular results releases per issuer; earlier preliminary/business updates are retained as exclusions, not extra quarters. No ninth-quarter replacement. The SEC release dateline determines publication day, not an unrelated earliest-event date on the 8-K cover. Duplicate same-day exhibits count once.
- Guidance is the latest source-supported quarterly range available by **15 calendar days before the regular result** under the frozen historical selection policy. Both issuance and SEC filing must be available. Explicit intervening updates/reaffirmations are recorded; identified earlier completed-period disclosures are excluded. No guide issued alongside the outcome is selected. The live entry window remains 17 days; that does not change this historical selection rule.
- SEC companyfacts retain original periods, concepts, accession, filed date, alternatives and compatible Q4 parent facts. No float amounts, mixed-concept subtraction, segment sums, or assumed calendar quarters. First source vintage is preferred and later competing values are retained. Where only a rounded GAAP release summary is available (Coherent, one Amplitude release), the original exact XBRL amount remains unchanged; rounding quantum and difference are recorded and a boundary-crossing interval blocks classification.
- Per-quarter source evidence includes the release/table headings, source URL/hash/retrieval time, selected range, actual, and filing versions in the companion JSON. Growth comparability is not a blanket outcome block. Acquisition and changed-basis questions specifically identified below remain blocked.
- All 2027 dates below are **unverified planning estimates**, calculated as the same-season 2026 SEC release date +364 days (same weekday). They are not announced dates or a precision claim. Conditional opening = estimated date minus17 calendar days at 00:00 America/New_York, displayed as the preceding day at21:00 Pacific. Actual first lock date is unknown until a qualifying announcement exists and all fresh checks pass.
- Announcement lead = publication date of the official date announcement to the actual SEC results-release date, in calendar days. A typical gap is the median of all four latest releases only. Partial medians are labelled observations, never substituted for a four-quarter typical gap. A median does not guarantee future lead time. Earliest practical locking is no earlier than BOTH the policy opening and availability of verified announcement evidence.

## Reproduction and safeguards

Research-only collectors reuse the existing SEC fetcher/cache/rate limiter. No database imports occur. Raw documents and request artifacts remain gitignored. The checked-in source selections and report are distinct from frozen v2 extraction, fresh evaluation sets, baseline observations and live-lock inputs. No experimental extractor was resumed and no LLM/provider calls were made.

```sh
.venv/bin/python -m earnings.research.scaling_2027q1_sources
.venv/bin/python -m earnings.research.scaling_2027q1_packets
.venv/bin/python -m earnings.research.scaling_2027q1_pairs
.venv/bin/python -m earnings.research.scaling_2027q1_report
```

The last command regenerates the JSON from pinned local source artifacts and checked-in agent selections. The first command may make rate-limited SEC requests; the report builder itself is offline. New source discoveries require explicit source curation; these tools do not automatically certify guidance semantics. The Markdown includes the same research snapshot and is intended for review.

## Operational conclusion

Prioritize Tier A for reviewed onboarding and schedule monitoring. Tier B has sufficient source-pair count but incomplete date-announcement evidence. Rambus needs additional directly issued consolidated quarterly history; component guidance and billings cannot be converted into an eligible total. No company was imported, scored, or locked in this research pass. All future live windows remain conditional on genuine announcements and unchanged current-state checks.
